### policy_compression_check function for validating existing compression policies

Source: https://github.com/timescale/timescaledb/blob/main/timescaledb/sql/policy_internal.sql

Defines the policy_compression_check function which validates compression policy configurations. This function can be used to check if an existing compression policy on a hypertable is valid. The function takes a JSONB config parameter and returns void, raising an error if validation fails.

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION _timescaledb_functions.policy_compression_check(config JSONB)
RETURNS void AS '@MODULE_PATHNAME@', 'ts_policy_compression_check'
LANGUAGE C;
```
```

> AGENT

Grounded now — and the docs change the picture in a way worth knowing before we commit:

**The turnkey "OTel → Postgres" path doesn't exist anymore.** The OTel Collector's only SQL-family *exporter* is **ClickHouse** (the thing you want to avoid). There's a `sqlqueryreceiver` for *reading* SQL as a telemetry source, but **no maintained Postgres exporter** — Promscale (the old "OTel in Postgres" answer) was sunset in 2023 and nothing replaced it. So "store OTel in Postgres" today means **custom code you own**, not a config flag. TimescaleDB gives you the right *storage* primitives (hypertables, retention, compression, even UUIDv7 partitioning — which pairs nicely with our `seq`/uuid7 usage) but not an OTel ingestion/view layer.

That said, **your instinct is sound for *this* service** — because the Hub is **low-volume**. It's infrequent webhooks + mostly-idle held SSE connections, not a high-RPS app. The usual "you need a columnar OLAP store for traces" argument doesn't apply. A relational table is genuinely fine here.

But two things bite, and they're the decision:

**1. Storing ≠ viewing.** Writing spans/logs to a table is easy. Rendering a *trace waterfall* is real UI work — the whole ecosystem (Jaeger/Tempo/Grafana) exists for that. "See their own OTel stack" could mean very different builds.

**2. ADR-0001 tension.** Self-host defaults to **SQLite**, not Postgres. If observability *requires* Postgres, you've reintroduced exactly the "extra backend overhead" you're trying to kill — just renamed.

## My recommended package

- **Always instrument with the OpenTelemetry Go SDK and expose `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT`.** Cheap, standard, vendor-neutral. Anyone who wants Grafana/Tempo/Jaeger/Datadog just points it there — *that's* "see their own OTel stack," literally, with zero opinion from us.
- **Plus a built-in lightweight store in the Hub's *own* DB** for the zero-infra self-hoster: a tiny custom OTLP-to-SQL writer into a few tables (spans, logs), **following the primary store** — SQLite (FTS5 for log search, JSON for attributes) by default, Postgres **+ optional TimescaleDB** (hypertable + retention/compression) when they've opted into Postgres. Built-in **retention/pruning** so it can't grow unbounded.
- **Viewing in v1: minimal.** Logs/traces are SQL-queryable + a basic in-app list/filter view (this is where an OpenTUI or web dashboard could later live). **Rich trace-waterfall UI is deferred** to the BYO-Grafana path — don't build a Jaeger clone for v1.
- **SQLite stays the self-host default** — telemetry never forces Postgres.

This is ADR-worthy once settled (it's surprising and hard-to-reverse), so I'll write **ADR-0008** after you decide.

**The one branching decision — what is the Hub's role for telemetry?**

### Minimal Development Setup (Single-Node SQLite)

Source: https://openobserve.ai/docs/enterprise-setup/terraform

Configure OpenObserve for local development using SQLite as the meta store and local cluster coordinator/queue store. No PostgreSQL or NATS are required.

```hcl
module "openobserve" {
  source  = "openobserve/openobserve/kubernetes"
  version = "~> 1.0"

  auth = {
    root_user_email    = "admin@example.com"
    root_user_password = "ChangeMe123!"
  }

  # Single-node local mode: no PostgreSQL or NATS required
  meta_store          = "sqlite"
  cluster_coordinator = "local"
  queue_store         = "local"
  nats                = { enabled = false }
}
```

--------------------------------

### POST /api/{organization}/v1/logs

Source: https://openobserve.ai/docs/reference/api/ingestion/logs/otlp

Ingest logs using the OpenTelemetry Protocol (OTLP). This endpoint accepts logs in JSON or Protobuf format and supports resource attributes and log records.

```APIDOC
## POST /api/{organization}/v1/logs

### Description
Ingest logs using the OpenTelemetry Protocol (OTLP). This endpoint accepts logs in JSON or Protobuf format and supports resource attributes and log records. A custom HTTP header `stream-name` can be used to specify a stream, defaulting to `default` if not provided.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/api/{organization}/v1/logs`

### Parameters
#### Headers
- **Content-Type** (string) - Required - `application/json` for JSON format or `application/x-protobuf` for Protobuf format.
- **stream-name** (string) - Optional - Specifies the target stream for the logs. Defaults to `default`.
- **Authorization** (string) - Required - Credentials for authentication. Format: `Basic <base64(user:password)>`.

#### Request Body
Supports OTLP log data model. Key fields include:
- **resourceLogs** (array) - Required - List of resource log groups.
  - **resource** (object) - Resource describing the entity producing the logs.
    - **attributes** (array) - Key-value pairs for resource-level metadata.
  - **scopeLogs** (array) - List of instrumentation scope log groups.
    - **scope** (object) - Instrumentation scope (library name and version).
    - **logRecords** (array) - List of individual log records.
      - **timeUnixNano** (string) - Required - Log timestamp in nanoseconds since Unix epoch.
      - **observedTimeUnixNano** (string) - Optional - Time the log was observed by the collector, in nanoseconds.
      - **severityNumber** (integer) - Optional - Numeric severity level (1–24).
      - **severityText** (string) - Optional - Human-readable severity string (e.g. `INFO`, `WARN`, `ERROR`).
      - **body** (object) - Log message body. Typically a `stringValue`.
      - **attributes** (array) - Key-value pairs for log-level metadata.
      - **traceId** (string) - Optional - Trace ID associated with this log record (hex string).
      - **spanId** (string) - Optional - Span ID associated with this log record (hex string).

### Request Example
```json
{
  "resourceLogs": [
    {
      "resource": {
        "attributes": [
          {
            "key": "service.name",
            "value": { "stringValue": "my-service" }
          },
          {
            "key": "service.version",
            "value": { "stringValue": "1.2.3" }
          },
          {
            "key": "host.name",
            "value": { "stringValue": "ip-10-2-50-35.us-east-2.compute.internal" }
          }
        ]
      },
      "scopeLogs": [
        {
          "scope": {
            "name": "my-logger",
            "version": "1.0.0"
          },
          "logRecords": [
            {
              "timeUnixNano": "1672149599212000000",
              "observedTimeUnixNano": "1672149599212000000",
              "severityNumber": 9,
              "severityText": "INFO",
              "body": {
                "stringValue": "Request processed successfully"
              },
              "attributes": [
                {
                  "key": "http.method",
                  "value": { "stringValue": "GET" }
                },
                {
                  "key": "http.status_code",
                  "value": { "intValue": "200" }
                },
                {
                  "key": "trace_id",
                  "value": { "stringValue": "4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736" }
                }
              ],
              "traceId": "4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736",
              "spanId": "00f067aa0ba902b7"
            }
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
An HTTP status `200 OK` with an empty `partialSuccess` object indicates all records were accepted.
```json
{
  "partialSuccess": {}
}
```
```

### Migrate from Grafana (LGTM Stack) to OpenObserve > What OpenObserve Changes

Source: https://openobserve.ai/docs/migration/migrate-from-grafana-to-openobserve

OpenObserve simplifies the observability stack by reducing the number of components to run from 4-6 in the LGTM stack to a single binary or Helm chart. It utilizes a single unified store for all signals (logs, metrics, traces) with options for local disk, S3, GCS, or Azure Blob storage, potentially lowering storage costs significantly. OpenObserve also offers native OpenTelemetry Protocol (OTLP) support for all signals via a single endpoint and includes built-in visualization, unlike the separate Grafana deployment required for LGTM.

--------------------------------

### Storage

Source: https://openobserve.ai/docs/administration/maintenance/storage-management/storage

OpenObserve stores two primary items: ingested stream data and metadata for that data. By default, metadata is always stored on disk using SQLite in Local mode, or PostgreSQL in Cluster mode. Stream data can be stored on disk or on object storage such as Amazon S3, MinIO, Google GCS, Alibaba OSS, or Tencent COS.

--------------------------------

### Architecture and Deployment Modes > Single-Node Mode > SQLite and Local Disk

Source: https://openobserve.ai/docs/architecture

Single-Node Mode can utilize either SQLite with local disk storage or SQLite with object storage. The default configuration with SQLite and local disk can ingest data at approximately 31 MB per second on an Apple M2 chip.
```

> AGENT

OpenObserve is confirmed as a near-perfect fit — and it actually serves your original goal *better* than Postgres would have. From its docs:

- **Single binary, single-node = SQLite metadata + local disk.** No PostgreSQL, no NATS, no etcd (those are cluster/HA-only). So it doesn't drag in the heavy backend you wanted to avoid — and it doesn't force Postgres on self-hosters, so **ADR-0001's SQLite default stands**.
- **Native OTLP** for logs *and* metrics *and* traces on one endpoint, with **built-in visualization** — which closes the "storing ≠ viewing" gap I flagged. It explicitly replaces the 4–6-component Grafana LGTM stack with one process.
- Ingests ~31 MB/s single-node on an M2 — vastly more than this low-volume Hub will ever produce.

So the resolved architecture: **the Hub instruments with the OTel Go SDK and emits OTLP to a configurable endpoint; the default self-host deployment bundles OpenObserve as that sink.** The Hub itself stays vendor-neutral (point `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` at OpenObserve, your own Grafana/Datadog, or nothing). Telemetry is **not** stored in the Hub's SQLite/Postgres — OpenObserve owns it (its own SQLite + local disk, or S3 at SaaS scale). No Prometheus/ClickHouse/Elastic anywhere.

This is hard-to-reverse and surprising (people expect Grafana/Prometheus), so I'm recording it as ADR-0008 with those sensible defaults — tell me if you'd rather *not* bundle it (recommend-only) or want S3 storage by default.

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/docs/adr/0008-observability-via-otel-and-bundled-openobserve.md",
  "content": "# 0008 — Observability via OpenTelemetry; bundled OpenObserve as the default self-host sink\n\n**Status:** Accepted\n**Date:** 2026-06-07\n\n## Decision\n\nThe Hub is instrumented with the **OpenTelemetry Go SDK** and emits traces, logs, and metrics over **OTLP** to a configurable endpoint (`OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT`). The Hub is **backend-agnostic** — it stores no telemetry in its own database.\n\nThe **default self-host deployment bundles [OpenObserve](https://openobserve.ai)** as the OTLP sink: a single binary that, in single-node mode, uses **SQLite metadata + local disk** (no PostgreSQL, NATS, or etcd — those are cluster/HA only), ingests all three signals on one native OTLP endpoint, and ships **built-in visualization** (log search, metrics, trace waterfalls). Users may instead point the Hub's OTLP exporter at their own OpenObserve, Grafana/Tempo, Datadog, etc., or disable export entirely. At SaaS scale OpenObserve can move stream data to object storage (S3/GCS) and to Postgres metadata in cluster mode.\n\nNo Prometheus, ClickHouse, Elasticsearch, or Grafana is required.\n\n## Why\n\nSelf-host is first-class (ADR-0001), so observability must add minimal overhead. OpenObserve collapses the 4–6-component LGTM stack into one lightweight binary with embedded SQLite + local disk — the same \"lean single artifact, scale to Postgres/object-store only when needed\" shape as the Hub itself, so it is philosophically consistent and doesn't reintroduce a heavy backend.\n\nStoring OTel in the Hub's own Postgres/SQLite (the original idea) was rejected: there is **no maintained OTel→Postgres exporter** (Promscale, the old answer, was sunset in 2023; the Collector's only SQL-family exporter is ClickHouse), so we would own a custom ingestion path **and** have to build a trace-waterfall UI from scratch. OpenObserve gives storage *and* the viewer for free, at lower total overhead, while keeping the Hub vendor-neutral through plain OTLP.\n\nKeeping the Hub OTLP-agnostic means \"see your own OTel stack\" is literal: whatever the user runs, they point the exporter at it.\n\n## Trade-offs accepted\n\n- The default self-host deployment runs **one extra process** (OpenObserve). Accepted: it is a single lightweight binary, far below Elastic/ClickHouse/LGTM overhead, and is optional/disable-able for a truly zero-sidecar run.\n- Telemetry retention/compression is governed by **OpenObserve**, not the Hub.\n- OpenObserve's *cluster/HA* mode needs PostgreSQL + NATS — but single-node self-host does not, so the SQLite-default promise (ADR-0001) holds for the common case.\n\n## Considered alternatives\n\n- **Store OTel in the Hub's Postgres/TimescaleDB:** rejected — no turnkey exporter post-Promscale; forces us to build a trace UI; would pressure Postgres-as-mandatory for self-host. Timescale's storage primitives are good but the ingestion+view layer is the missing, expensive part.\n- **Emit OTLP only, bring-your-own backend:** kept as the escape hatch, not the default — a zero-infra self-hoster would otherwise see nothing out of the box.\n- **Grafana LGTM (Loki/Tempo/Mimir/Grafana) or Prometheus+Tempo+Loki:** rejected — 4–6 components, heavy for self-host.\n- **ClickHouse-based (SigNoz / native ClickHouse exporter):** rejected — reintroduces the columnar-OLAP backend overhead we set out to avoid.\n"
}
```

## Resolved: observability
- **Hub** → OpenTelemetry Go SDK, emits OTLP to a configurable endpoint; stores **no** telemetry itself (vendor-neutral).
- **Default self-host sink** → **OpenObserve** bundled in `docker compose`: one binary, single-node SQLite + local disk, native OTLP for traces/logs/metrics, built-in UI. No Prometheus/ClickHouse/Elastic/Grafana, no forced Postgres — so ADR-0001's SQLite default holds.
- **BYO** → point `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` anywhere, or unset to disable.
- Recorded in **ADR-0008**; README gained a **Observability** section + updated Deploy modes.

Your original "in Postgres itself" goal is actually *better* served this way — OpenObserve is lighter than Postgres+Timescale+Grafana would have been, and gives the trace-waterfall UI for free (which a Postgres approach would've forced us to build, since Promscale's death left no turnkey OTel→Postgres path).

  "content": "-- Deduplication of GitHub webhook deliveries by X-GitHub-Delivery id.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS deliveries (\n    id          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    event       TEXT NOT NULL,\n    received_at INTEGER NOT NULL\n);\n\n-- A Round is the lifecycle of a single PR head SHA (owner/repo#number @ sha).\n-- A new head SHA is a new Round; see ADR-0004.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rounds (\n    owner      TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    repo       TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    number     INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    sha        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    PRIMARY KEY (owner, repo, number, sha)\n);\n\n-- Latest-state pending store: at most one row per PR per signal-type, the\n-- newest summary wins (ADR-0006). Nothing here is garbage-collected; a newer\n-- same-type event overwrites the row.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pending (\n    owner       TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    repo        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    number      INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    signal_type TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    sha         TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    pr_state    TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    summary     TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at  INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    PRIMARY KEY (owner, repo, number, signal_type)\n);\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/store/store.go",
  "content": "// Package store is the Hub's SQLite-backed persistence layer.\n//\n// It uses modernc.org/sqlite — a pure-Go (cgo-free) driver — so the Hub\n// compiles to a single static binary (ADR-0001).\npackage store\n\nimport (\n\t\"database/sql\"\n\t_ \"embed\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t_ \"modernc.org/sqlite\"\n)\n\n//go:embed schema.sql\nvar schema string\n\n// Store wraps the SQLite database.\ntype Store struct {\n\tdb *sql.DB\n}\n\n// Open opens (creating if needed) the database at path and applies the schema.\nfunc Open(path string) (*Store, error) {\n\tdb, err := sql.Open(\"sqlite\", path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"open sqlite: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := db.Exec(schema); err != nil {\n\t\t_ = db.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"apply schema: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn &Store{db: db}, nil\n}\n\n// Close closes the underlying database.\nfunc (s *Store) Close() error { return s.db.Close() }\n\n// SeenDelivery records a GitHub delivery id and reports whether it is new.\n// It returns true the first time an id is seen, false for duplicates.\nfunc (s *Store) SeenDelivery(id, event string) (bool, error) {\n\tres, err := s.db.Exec(\n\t\t`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO deliveries (id, event, received_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\tid, event, time.Now().Unix(),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"record delivery: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tn, err := res.RowsAffected()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"delivery rows: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn n == 1, nil\n}\n\n// RecordRound upserts the latest-seen timestamp for a PR head SHA (a Round).\nfunc (s *Store) RecordRound(owner, repo string, number int, sha string) error {\n\t_, err := s.db.Exec(\n\t\t`INSERT INTO rounds (owner, repo, number, sha, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(owner, repo, number, sha) DO UPDATE SET updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,\n\t\towner, repo, number, sha, time.Now().Unix(),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"record round: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n"
}

Different event types carry the PR number and\n// head SHA in different places; the optional pointers reflect that.\ntype Envelope struct {\n\tAction     string `json:\"action\"`\n\tNumber     int    `json:\"number\"`\n\tRepository struct {\n\t\tName  string `json:\"name\"`\n\t\tOwner struct {\n\t\t\tLogin string `json:\"login\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"owner\"`\n\t} `json:\"repository\"`\n\tPullRequest *struct {\n\t\tNumber int    `json:\"number\"`\n\t\tState  string `json:\"state\"`\n\t\tHead   struct {\n\t\t\tSHA string `json:\"sha\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"head\"`\n\t} `json:\"pull_request\"`\n\tCheckSuite *struct {\n\t\tHeadSHA      string `json:\"head_sha\"`\n\t\tPullRequests []struct {\n\t\t\tNumber int `json:\"number\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"pull_requests\"`\n\t} `json:\"check_suite\"`\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/hub/round.go",
  "content": "package hub\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/github\"\n)\n\n// RoundKey identifies one settle lifecycle: a PR at a specific head SHA.\ntype RoundKey struct {\n\tOwner  string\n\tRepo   string\n\tNumber int\n\tSHA    string\n}\n\n// String renders the canonical key form owner/repo#number@sha.\nfunc (k RoundKey) String() string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s#%d@%s\", k.Owner, k.Repo, k.Number, k.SHA)\n}\n\n// DeriveRound extracts a RoundKey from a webhook envelope. ok is false when the\n// event does not pertain to a specific PR head SHA, in which case the caller\n// skips bucketing.\nfunc DeriveRound(env github.Envelope) (key RoundKey, ok bool) {\n\towner := env.Repository.Owner.Login\n\trepo := env.Repository.Name\n\tif owner == \"\" || repo == \"\" {\n\t\treturn RoundKey{}, false\n\t}\n\tswitch {\n\tcase env.PullRequest != nil && env.PullRequest.Head.SHA != \"\":\n\t\treturn RoundKey{owner, repo, env.PullRequest.Number, env.PullRequest.Head.SHA}, true\n\tcase env.CheckSuite != nil && env.CheckSuite.HeadSHA != \"\" && len(env.CheckSuite.PullRequests) > 0:\n\t\treturn RoundKey{owner, repo, env.CheckSuite.PullRequests[0].Number, env.CheckSuite.HeadSHA}, true\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn RoundKey{}, false\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/hub/webhook.go",
  "content": "// Package hub implements the Caw Hub's HTTP-facing logic: webhook ingest,\n// signature verification, delivery dedupe, and Round bucketing.\npackage hub\n\nimport (\n\t\"crypto/hmac\"\n\t\"crypto/sha256\"\n\t\"encoding/hex\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"log\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/gin-gonic/gin\"\n\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/github\"\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/store\"\n)\n\n// maxBody caps the webhook payload we read (GitHub's documented ceiling is 25 MiB).\nconst maxBody = 25 << 20\n\n// Hub holds the dependencies for handling webhooks.\ntype Hub struct {\n\tstore  *store.Store\n\tsecret []byte\n}\n\n// New constructs a Hub backed by st, verifying signatures with secret.\nfunc New(st *store.Store, secret []byte) *Hub {\n\treturn &Hub{store: st, secret: secret}\n}\n\n// VerifySignature reports whether sigHeader (\"sha256=<hex>\") is a valid\n// HMAC-SHA256 of payload under secret. The comparison is constant-time.\nfunc VerifySignature(secret, payload []byte, sigHeader string) bool {\n\tconst prefix = \"sha256=\"\n\tif !strings.HasPrefix(sigHeader, prefix) {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\twant, err := hex.DecodeString(sigHeader[len(prefix):])\n\tif err != nil || len(want) != sha256.Size {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tmac := hmac.New(sha256.New, secret)\n\tmac.Write(payload)\n\treturn hmac.Equal(want, mac.Sum(nil))\n}\n\n// HandleWebhook is the POST /webhooks/github handler. It verifies the\n// signature, dedupes by delivery id, then buckets the event into a Round.\nfunc (h *Hub) HandleWebhook(c *gin.Context) {\n\tbody, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(c.Request.Body, maxBody))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tc.String(http.StatusBadRequest, \"read body\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif len(h.secret) == 0 || !VerifySignature(h.secret, body, c.GetHeader(\"X-Hub-Signature-256\")) {\n\t\tc.String(http.StatusUnauthorized, \"invalid signature\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tevent := c.GetHeader(\"X-GitHub-Event\")\n\tif delivery := c.GetHeader(\"X-GitHub-Delivery\"); delivery != \"\" {\n\t\tisNew, err := h.store.SeenDelivery(delivery, event)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"dedupe delivery %s: %v\", delivery, err)\n\t\t\tc.String(http.StatusInternalServerError, \"dedupe\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !isNew {\n\t\t\tc.String(http.StatusOK, \"duplicate\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar env github.Envelope\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(body, &env); err != nil {\n\t\tc.String(http.StatusBadRequest, \"parse payload\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif key, ok := DeriveRound(env); ok {\n\t\tif err := h.store.RecordRound(key.Owner, key.Repo, key.Number, key.SHA); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"record round %s: %v\", key, err)\n\t\t\tc.String(http.StatusInternalServerError, \"record round\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"bucketed %q into round %s\", event, key)\n\t}\n\n\tc.String(http.StatusAccepted, \"accepted\")\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/hub/webhook_test.go",
  "content": "package hub\n\nimport (\n\t\"crypto/hmac\"\n\t\"crypto/sha256\"\n\t\"encoding/hex\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/github\"\n)\n\nfunc sign(secret, payload []byte) string {\n\tmac := hmac.New(sha256.New, secret)\n\tmac.Write(payload)\n\treturn \"sha256=\" + hex.EncodeToString(mac.Sum(nil))\n}\n\nfunc TestVerifySignature(t *testing.T) {\n\tsecret := []byte(\"s3cr3t\")\n\tpayload := []byte(`{\"hello\":\"world\"}`)\n\tgood := sign(secret, payload)\n\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname   string\n\t\tsecret []byte\n\t\tbody   []byte\n\t\theader string\n\t\twant   bool\n\t}{\n\t\t{\"valid\", secret, payload, good, true},\n\t\t{\"wrong secret\", []byte(\"nope\"), payload, good, false},\n\t\t{\"tampered body\", secret, []byte(`{\"hello\":\"evil\"}`), good, false},\n\t\t{\"missing prefix\", secret, payload, hex.EncodeToString([]byte(\"x\")), false},\n\t\t{\"empty header\", secret, payload, \"\", false},\n\t\t{\"not hex\", secret, payload, \"sha256=zzzz\", false},\n\t\t{\"wrong length\", secret, payload, \"sha256=ab\", false},\n\t}\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tif got := VerifySignature(tt.secret, tt.body, tt.header); got != tt.want {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"VerifySignature(%q) = %v, want %v\", tt.name, got, tt.want)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestDeriveRound(t *testing.T) {\n\tmk := func() github.Envelope {\n\t\tvar e github.Envelope\n\t\te.Repository.Owner.Login = \"ravencloak-org\"\n\t\te.Repository.Name = \"caw\"\n\t\treturn e\n\t}\n\n\tt.Run(\"from pull_request\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\te := mk()\n\t\te.PullRequest = &struct {\n\t\t\tNumber int    `json:\"number\"`\n\t\t\tState  string `json:\"state\"`\n\t\t\tHead   struct {\n\t\t\t\tSHA string `json:\"sha\"`\n\t\t\t} `json:\"head\"`\n\t\t}{Number: 42}\n\t\te.PullRequest.Head.SHA = \"deadbeef\"\n\t\tkey, ok := DeriveRound(e)\n\t\tif !ok || key.String() != \"ravencloak-org/caw#42@deadbeef\" {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"got %v ok=%v\", key, ok)\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"from check_suite\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\te := mk()\n\t\te.CheckSuite = &struct {\n\t\t\tHeadSHA      string `json:\"head_sha\"`\n\t\t\tPullRequests []struct {\n\t\t\t\tNumber int `json:\"number\"`\n\t\t\t} `json:\"pull_requests\"`\n\t\t}{HeadSHA: \"cafef00d\", PullRequests: []struct {\n\t\t\tNumber int `json:\"number\"`\n\t\t}{{Number: 7}}}\n\t\tkey, ok := DeriveRound(e)\n\t\tif !ok || key.String() != \"ravencloak-org/caw#7@cafef00d\" {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"got %v ok=%v\", key, ok)\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"no PR context\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tif _, ok := DeriveRound(mk()); ok {\n\t\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected ok=false when no PR/check_suite present\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/cmd/hub/main.go",
  "content": "// Command hub is the Caw Hub: it ingests GitHub webhooks, compiles PR feedback\n// into summaries, and (in later slices) pushes them to Watchers over SSE.\n//\n// Slice 1 wires the HTTP server, webhook signature verification, delivery\n// dedupe, Round bucketing, and the SQLite store.\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"log\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/signal\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/gin-gonic/gin\"\n\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/config\"\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/hub\"\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/store\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n\tcfg := config.Load()\n\n\tst, err := store.Open(cfg.DatabasePath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Fatalf(\"open store: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer func() { _ = st.Close() }()\n\n\tif cfg.GitHubWebhookSecret == \"\" {\n\t\tlog.Println(\"warning: CAW_GH_WEBHOOK_SECRET is empty; all webhooks will be rejected\")\n\t}\n\n\tr := gin.New()\n\tr.Use(gin.Logger(), gin.Recovery())\n\n\th := hub.New(st, []byte(cfg.GitHubWebhookSecret))\n\tr.POST(\"/webhooks/github\", h.HandleWebhook)\n\tr.GET(\"/healthz\", func(c *gin.Context) { c.String(http.StatusOK, \"ok\") })\n\n\tsrv := &http.Server{\n\t\tAddr:              cfg.Addr,\n\t\tHandler:           r,\n\t\tReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second,\n\t}\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"hub listening on %s\", cfg.Addr)\n\t\tif err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {\n\t\t\tlog.Fatalf(\"listen: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\tctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)\n\tdefer stop()\n\t<-ctx.Done()\n\n\tlog.Println(\"shutting down\")\n\tshutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\tif err := srv.Shutdown(shutdownCtx); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"shutdown: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\t_ = os.Stdout.Sync()\n}\n"
}

1	# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

3	# ---- Builder ---------------------------------------------------------------
4	# Pure-Go build (modernc.org/sqlite) => CGO_ENABLED=0, no C toolchain needed.
5	FROM golang:1.23 AS builder
6	
7	WORKDIR /src
8	
9	# Cache module downloads as their own layer.
10	COPY go.mod go.sum ./
11	RUN go mod download
12	
13	# Build the static hub binary.
14	COPY . .
15	RUN CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux go build \
16	    -trimpath \
17	    -ldflags="-s -w" \
18	    -o /out/hub ./cmd/hub
19	
20	# ---- Final -----------------------------------------------------------------
21	# distroless/static: no shell, no libc — just the static binary. nonroot user.
22	FROM gcr.io/distroless/static-debian12:nonroot
23	
24	WORKDIR /
25	
26	# Static binary only — keeps the image tiny.
27	COPY --from=builder /out/hub /hub
28	
29	USER nonroot:nonroot
30	
31	EXPOSE 8080
32	
33	ENTRYPOINT ["/hub"]

## Shipped (parallel: Hub core by me, deployment + CI by the two background agents)

**Hub core** (Go + Gin + `modernc.org/sqlite`, cgo-free static binary):
- `POST /webhooks/github` → constant-time HMAC-SHA256 signature verify → `X-GitHub-Delivery` dedupe → Round bucketing by `owner/repo#number@sha`
- SQLite store: `deliveries` (dedupe), `rounds`, latest-state `pending` skeleton (ADR-0006)
- graceful shutdown, `/healthz`
- tests pass (`go test -race ./...`): signature verify (valid/tamper/wrong-secret/malformed) + `DeriveRound`

**Deployment** (agent): multi-stage `Dockerfile` (distroless static), `docker-compose.yml` (hub + bundled OpenObserve per ADR-0008), `.dockerignore`, `.env.example`.
**CI/hygiene** (agent): GitHub Actions (build/vet/`test -race` + golangci-lint), `.golangci.yml`, dependabot (gomod/actions/docker), `Makefile`.

Layout:
```
cmd/hub/main.go
internal/{config,store,github,hub}/…
Dockerfile  docker-compose.yml  .github/  Makefile
```

- I aligned Go to **1.25** everywhere (a dep's minimum) — CI and Dockerfile updated to match.

  "new_string": "\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"record round: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// RoundExists reports whether a Round row exists for the given key.\nfunc (s *Store) RoundExists(owner, repo string, number int, sha string) (bool, error) {\n\tvar n int\n\tif err := s.db.QueryRow(\n\t\t`SELECT COUNT(1) FROM rounds WHERE owner = ? AND repo = ? AND number = ? AND sha = ?`,\n\t\towner, repo, number, sha,\n\t).Scan(&n); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"round exists: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn n > 0, nil\n}\n\n// PendingItem is a stored latest-state summary for one PR signal-type (ADR-0006).\ntype PendingItem struct {\n\tOwner      string\n\tRepo       string\n\tNumber     int\n\tSignalType string\n\tSHA        string\n\tPRState    string\n\tSummary    string\n\tUpdatedAt  int64\n}\n\n// UpsertPending stores p as the latest pending item for its\n// (owner, repo, number, signal_type); a newer write replaces the prior one\n// (ADR-0006). The stored timestamp is set to now.\nfunc (s *Store) UpsertPending(p PendingItem) error {\n\t_, err := s.db.Exec(\n\t\t`INSERT INTO pending (owner, repo, number, signal_type, sha, pr_state, summary, updated_at)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(owner, repo, number, signal_type) DO UPDATE SET\n\t\t     sha = excluded.sha, pr_state = excluded.pr_state,\n\t\t     summary = excluded.summary, updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,\n\t\tp.Owner, p.Repo, p.Number, p.SignalType, p.SHA, p.PRState, p.Summary, time.Now().Unix(),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"upsert pending: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ListPending returns all stored pending items in a stable order.\nfunc (s *Store) ListPending() ([]PendingItem, error) {\n\trows, err := s.db.Query(\n\t\t`SELECT owner, repo, number, signal_type, sha, pr_state, summary, updated_at\n\t\t FROM pending ORDER BY owner, repo, number, signal_type`,\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"list pending: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer func() { _ = rows.Close() }()\n\n\tvar items []PendingItem\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar p PendingItem\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(\n\t\t\t&p.Owner, &p.Repo, &p.Number, &p.SignalType,\n\t\t\t&p.SHA, &p.PRState, &p.Summary, &p.UpdatedAt,\n\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"scan pending: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\titems = append(items, p)\n\t}\n\treturn items, rows.Err()\n}"
}

  "content": "package store\n\nimport (\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"testing\"\n)\n\nfunc newTestStore(t *testing.T) *Store {\n\tt.Helper()\n\ts, err := Open(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), \"test.db\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Open: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tt.Cleanup(func() { _ = s.Close() })\n\treturn s\n}\n\nfunc TestOpenAppliesSchemaIdempotently(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\tp := filepath.Join(dir, \"x.db\")\n\ts1, err := Open(p)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"first open: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\t_ = s1.Close()\n\t// Re-opening runs the schema again; IF NOT EXISTS makes this a no-op.\n\ts2, err := Open(p)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"second open: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\t_ = s2.Close()\n}\n\nfunc TestOpenBadPath(t *testing.T) {\n\tif _, err := Open(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), \"nope\", \"missing\", \"x.db\")); err == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected error opening db in nonexistent directory\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestSeenDelivery(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := newTestStore(t)\n\n\tisNew, err := s.SeenDelivery(\"d1\", \"push\")\n\tif err != nil || !isNew {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"first SeenDelivery = (%v, %v), want (true, nil)\", isNew, err)\n\t}\n\tisNew, err = s.SeenDelivery(\"d1\", \"push\")\n\tif err != nil || isNew {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"duplicate SeenDelivery = (%v, %v), want (false, nil)\", isNew, err)\n\t}\n\tisNew, err = s.SeenDelivery(\"d2\", \"pull_request\")\n\tif err != nil || !isNew {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"new SeenDelivery = (%v, %v), want (true, nil)\", isNew, err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRecordRoundAndExists(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := newTestStore(t)\n\n\texists, err := s.RoundExists(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha1\")\n\tif err != nil || exists {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"RoundExists before = (%v, %v), want (false, nil)\", exists, err)\n\t}\n\tif err := s.RecordRound(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha1\"); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"RecordRound: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\t// Upsert of the same key must not error or duplicate.\n\tif err := s.RecordRound(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha1\"); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"RecordRound (upsert): %v\", err)\n\t}\n\texists, err = s.RoundExists(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha1\")\n\tif err != nil || !exists {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"RoundExists after = (%v, %v), want (true, nil)\", exists, err)\n\t}\n\t// A different SHA is a different Round.\n\texists, _ = s.RoundExists(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha2\")\n\tif exists {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"RoundExists for different SHA should be false\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestPendingLatestWinsPerSignalType(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := newTestStore(t)\n\n\tmk := func(sig, sha, summary string) PendingItem {\n\t\treturn PendingItem{Owner: \"o\", Repo: \"r\", Number: 1, SignalType: sig, SHA: sha, PRState: \"open\", Summary: summary}\n\t}\n\tif err := s.UpsertPending(mk(\"checks\", \"a\", \"first\")); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif err := s.UpsertPending(mk(\"checks\", \"b\", \"second\")); err != nil { // replaces \"first\"\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif err := s.UpsertPending(mk(\"comments\", \"a\", \"a comment\")); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\titems, err := s.ListPending()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ListPending: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(items) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"got %d items, want 2 (one per signal-type)\", len(items))\n\t}\n\tbyType := map[string]PendingItem{}\n\tfor _, it := range items {\n\t\tbyType[it.SignalType] = it\n\t}\n\tif got := byType[\"checks\"]; got.Summary != \"second\" || got.SHA != \"b\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"checks = %+v, want latest (summary=second, sha=b)\", got)\n\t}\n\tif got := byType[\"comments\"]; got.Summary != \"a comment\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"comments = %+v\", got)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestListPendingEmpty(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := newTestStore(t)\n\titems, err := s.ListPending()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ListPending: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(items) != 0 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"got %d, want 0\", len(items))\n\t}\n}\n"

  "content": "package github\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"testing\"\n)\n\nfunc TestUnmarshalPullRequestEnvelope(t *testing.T) {\n\tdata := []byte(`{\n\t\t\"action\": \"synchronize\",\n\t\t\"number\": 42,\n\t\t\"repository\": {\"name\": \"caw\", \"owner\": {\"login\": \"ravencloak-org\"}},\n\t\t\"pull_request\": {\"number\": 42, \"state\": \"open\", \"head\": {\"sha\": \"deadbeef\"}}\n\t}`)\n\n\tvar e Envelope\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &e); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unmarshal: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif e.Action != \"synchronize\" || e.Repository.Owner.Login != \"ravencloak-org\" || e.Repository.Name != \"caw\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"envelope basics wrong: %+v\", e)\n\t}\n\tif e.PullRequest == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"PullRequest should be non-nil\")\n\t}\n\tif e.PullRequest.Number != 42 || e.PullRequest.Head.SHA != \"deadbeef\" || e.PullRequest.State != \"open\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"pull_request fields wrong: %+v\", *e.PullRequest)\n\t}\n\tif e.CheckSuite != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"CheckSuite should be nil for a pull_request payload\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUnmarshalCheckSuiteEnvelope(t *testing.T) {\n\tdata := []byte(`{\n\t\t\"action\": \"completed\",\n\t\t\"repository\": {\"name\": \"caw\", \"owner\": {\"login\": \"ravencloak-org\"}},\n\t\t\"check_suite\": {\"head_sha\": \"cafef00d\", \"pull_requests\": [{\"number\": 7}]}\n\t}`)\n\n\tvar e Envelope\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &e); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unmarshal: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif e.CheckSuite == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"CheckSuite should be non-nil\")\n\t}\n\tif e.CheckSuite.HeadSHA != \"cafef00d\" || len(e.CheckSuite.PullRequests) != 1 || e.CheckSuite.PullRequests[0].Number != 7 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"check_suite fields wrong: %+v\", *e.CheckSuite)\n\t}\n\tif e.PullRequest != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"PullRequest should be nil for a check_suite payload\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUnmarshalNonPREnvelope(t *testing.T) {\n\t// e.g. a ping event: no pull_request / check_suite.\n\tdata := []byte(`{\"zen\": \"Keep it simple\", \"repository\": {\"name\": \"caw\", \"owner\": {\"login\": \"o\"}}}`)\n\tvar e Envelope\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &e); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unmarshal: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif e.PullRequest != nil || e.CheckSuite != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"ping envelope should have neither pull_request nor check_suite\")\n\t}\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package hub\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"net/http/httptest\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\n\t\"github.com/gin-gonic/gin\"\n\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/store\"\n)\n\nfunc init() { gin.SetMode(gin.TestMode) }\n\nfunc newHarness(t *testing.T, secret []byte) (*gin.Engine, *store.Store) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tst, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), \"h.db\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"open store: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tt.Cleanup(func() { _ = st.Close() })\n\tr := gin.New()\n\tr.POST(\"/webhooks/github\", New(st, secret).HandleWebhook)\n\treturn r, st\n}\n\nfunc post(r *gin.Engine, body []byte, headers map[string]string) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {\n\treq := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, \"/webhooks/github\", bytes.NewReader(body))\n\tfor k, v := range headers {\n\t\treq.Header.Set(k, v)\n\t}\n\tw := httptest.NewRecorder()\n\tr.ServeHTTP(w, req)\n\treturn w\n}\n\nvar prBody = []byte(`{\"action\":\"opened\",\"repository\":{\"name\":\"caw\",\"owner\":{\"login\":\"ravencloak-org\"}},\"pull_request\":{\"number\":42,\"state\":\"open\",\"head\":{\"sha\":\"deadbeef\"}}}`)\n\nfunc TestHandleWebhook_ValidBucketsRound(t *testing.T) {\n\tsecret := []byte(\"s3cr3t\")\n\tr, st := newHarness(t, secret)\n\n\tw := post(r, prBody, map[string]string{\n\t\t\"X-Hub-Signature-256\": sign(secret, prBody),\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Event\":      \"pull_request\",\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Delivery\":   \"del-1\",\n\t})\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusAccepted {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"status = %d, want 202; body=%q\", w.Code, w.Body.String())\n\t}\n\texists, err := st.RoundExists(\"ravencloak-org\", \"caw\", 42, \"deadbeef\")\n\tif err != nil || !exists {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"round not recorded (exists=%v err=%v)\", exists, err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestHandleWebhook_DuplicateDelivery(t *testing.T) {\n\tsecret := []byte(\"s3cr3t\")\n\tr, _ := newHarness(t, secret)\n\thdr := map[string]string{\n\t\t\"X-Hub-Signature-256\": sign(secret, prBody),\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Event\":      \"pull_request\",\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Delivery\":   \"dup\",\n\t}\n\tif w := post(r, prBody, hdr); w.Code != http.StatusAccepted {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"first status = %d, want 202\", w.Code)\n\t}\n\tw := post(r, prBody, hdr)\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusOK || w.Body.String() != \"duplicate\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"second status = %d body=%q, want 200 duplicate\", w.Code, w.Body.String())\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestHandleWebhook_BadSignature(t *testing.T) {\n\tr, st := newHarness(t, []byte(\"s3cr3t\"))\n\tw := post(r, prBody, map[string]string{\n\t\t\"X-Hub-Signature-256\": sign([]byte(\"wrong\"), prBody),\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Event\":      \"pull_request\",\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Delivery\":   \"del-x\",\n\t})\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"status = %d, want 401\", w.Code)\n\t}\n\tif exists, _ := st.RoundExists(\"ravencloak-org\", \"caw\", 42, \"deadbeef\"); exists {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"round must not be recorded on bad signature\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestHandleWebhook_EmptySecretRejects(t *testing.T) {\n\tr, _ := newHarness(t, nil)\n\tw := post(r, prBody, map[string]string{\n\t\t\"X-Hub-Signature-256\": sign([]byte(\"\"), prBody),\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Event\":      \"pull_request\",\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Delivery\":   \"del-y\",\n\t})\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"status = %d, want 401 when secret unset\", w.Code)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestHandleWebhook_NonPREventNoRound(t *testing.T) {\n\tsecret := []byte(\"s3cr3t\")\n\tr, st := newHarness(t, secret)\n\tping := []byte(`{\"zen\":\"x\",\"repository\":{\"name\":\"caw\",\"owner\":{\"login\":\"ravencloak-org\"}}}`)\n\tw := post(r, ping, map[string]string{\n\t\t\"X-Hub-Signature-256\": sign(secret, ping),\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Event\":      \"ping\",\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Delivery\":   \"del-ping\",\n\t})\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusAccepted {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"status = %d, want 202\", w.Code)\n\t}\n\tif exists, _ := st.RoundExists(\"ravencloak-org\", \"caw\", 0, \"\"); exists {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"no round should be recorded for a non-PR event\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestHandleWebhook_MalformedJSON(t *testing.T) {\n\tsecret := []byte(\"s3cr3t\")\n\tr, _ := newHarness(t, secret)\n\tbad := []byte(`{not json`)\n\tw := post(r, bad, map[string]string{\n\t\t\"X-Hub-Signature-256\": sign(secret, bad),\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Event\":      \"pull_request\",\n\t\t\"X-GitHub-Delivery\":   \"del-bad\",\n\t})\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusBadRequest {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"status = %d, want 400\", w.Code)\n\t}\n}\n"
}

**What's tested:** config defaults+overrides; store dedupe / round upsert+exists / pending latest-wins / error paths after `Close`; envelope unmarshal (pull_request, check_suite, ping); and the full `HandleWebhook` HTTP surface — valid→202+round, duplicate→200, bad-sig→401, empty-secret→401, non-PR→202 no-round, malformed→400, store-error→500.

## One manual step you'll need for Codecov
Uploads (and the badge) need the repo activated on Codecov with a token — I can't do this part, it requires your Codecov account:
1. Log into [codecov.io](https://codecov.io) with the `ravencloak-org` GitHub org and add the `caw` repo.
2. Copy its upload token and add it as a repo secret:
   `! gh secret set CODECOV_TOKEN --repo ravencloak-org/caw` (paste the token when prompted)

The CI step uses `fail_ci_if_error: false`, so CI stays green even before the token exists — the badge will just read "unknown" until the first upload lands. Public-repo PR uploads can work tokenless too, but the token avoids rate-limit flakiness.

  "content": "name: CI\n\non:\n  push:\n    branches: [main]\n  pull_request:\n    branches: [main]\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\n# Cancel an in-flight run for the same ref when a new commit arrives.\nconcurrency:\n  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}\n  cancel-in-progress: true\n\nenv:\n  CGO_ENABLED: \"0\"\n\njobs:\n  build:\n    name: Build\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: actions/setup-go@v5\n        with:\n          go-version: \"1.25\"\n          cache: true\n      - run: go build ./...\n\n  vet:\n    name: Vet\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: actions/setup-go@v5\n        with:\n          go-version: \"1.25\"\n          cache: true\n      - run: go vet ./...\n\n  test:\n    name: Test\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: actions/setup-go@v5\n        with:\n          go-version: \"1.25\"\n          cache: true\n      - name: Test with coverage\n        run: go test -race -covermode=atomic -coverprofile=coverage.out ./...\n      - name: Upload coverage to Codecov\n        uses: codecov/codecov-action@v5\n        with:\n          files: ./coverage.out\n          token: ${{ secrets.CODECOV_TOKEN }}\n          slug: ravencloak-org/caw\n          fail_ci_if_error: false\n\n  lint:\n    name: Lint\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: actions/setup-go@v5\n        with:\n          go-version: \"1.25\"\n          cache: true\n      - uses: golangci/golangci-lint-action@v6\n        with:\n          version: latest\n"

```
### Example Configuration

Source: https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-collector-contrib/blob/main/exporter/opensearchexporter/README.md

This configuration demonstrates how to set up the OpenSearch exporter with an HTTP endpoint, define log and trace index patterns using service name and environment placeholders, specify fallback values, and configure the sending queue.

```yaml
exporters:
  opensearch:
    http:
      endpoint: http://opensearch.example.com:9200
    # Logs configuration
    logs_index: "otel-logs-%{service.name}-%{env}"
    logs_index_fallback: "default"
    logs_index_time_format: "yyyy.MM.dd"
    # Traces configuration
    traces_index: "otel-traces-%{service.name}-%{deployment.environment}"
    traces_index_fallback: "unknown"
    traces_index_time_format: "yyyy.MM.dd"
    sending_queue:
      batch:
```

--------------------------------

### Encrypted Password in YAML Configuration

Source: https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-collector-contrib/blob/main/confmap/provider/aesprovider/README.md

Example of using an AES placeholder for a password in a YAML configuration.

```yaml
password: ${aes:REDACTED}
```

### Configuration options > Dynamic Indexing

Source: https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-collector-contrib/blob/main/exporter/opensearchexporter/README.md

**Placeholder Syntax:**
- Placeholder: `%{key}`
  - Example: `otel-logs-%{service.name}` or `otel-traces-%{service.name}-%{env}`
  - Multiple placeholders are supported per index name
  - The value is looked up from item attributes (log/span), scope attributes, and resource attributes (in that precedence order)
  - If the key is missing, the fallback value is used
  - Generated index names must adhere to [OpenSearch index naming restrictions](https://docs.opensearch.org/latest/api-reference/index-apis/create-index/#index-naming-restrictions)

--------------------------------

### Configuration options > Dynamic Indexing

Source: https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-collector-contrib/blob/main/exporter/opensearchexporter/README.md

**Configuration Options:**
- `logs_index` - Custom index name pattern for logs
- `logs_index_fallback` - Fallback value when placeholder is missing (default: `unknown`)
- `logs_index_time_format` - Time suffix format for logs
- `traces_index` - Custom index name pattern for traces
- `traces_index_fallback` - Fallback value when placeholder is missing (default: `unknown`)
- `traces_index_time_format` - Time suffix format for traces

--------------------------------

### How it works

Source: https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-collector-contrib/blob/main/confmap/provider/aesprovider/README.md

Use placeholders with the following pattern ${aes:<encrypted & base64-encoded value>} in a configuration. The value will be decrypted using the AES key provided in the environment variable OTEL_AES_CREDENTIAL_PROVIDER
```

### Add `version` property to configuration

Source: https://github.com/golangci/golangci-lint/blob/main/docs/content/docs/product/migration-guide.md

The `version` property has been added to the configuration file to specify the configuration schema version.

```yaml
version: "2"
```

--------------------------------

### Migrate linters to formatters section

Source: https://github.com/golangci/golangci-lint/blob/main/docs/content/docs/product/migration-guide.md

This tabbed example demonstrates the migration of linters like `gci`, `gofmt`, `gofumpt`, and `goimports` from the `linters.enable` list in v1 to the `formatters.enable` section in v2.

```yaml
linters:
  enable:
    - gci
    - gofmt
    - gofumpt
    - goimports
```

```yaml
formatters:
  enable:
    - gci
    - gofmt
    - gofumpt
    - goimports
```

--------------------------------

### Configure Custom Linter in .golangci.yml

Source: https://github.com/golangci/golangci-lint/blob/main/docs/content/docs/plugins/go-plugins.md

Example configuration for a custom linter within the `.golangci.yml` file. It specifies the path to the plugin, description, original URL, and optional settings.

```yaml
version: "2"

linters:
  settings:
    custom:
      example:
        path: /example.so
        description: The description of the linter
        original-url: github.com/golangci/example-linter
        settings: # Settings are optional.
          one: Foo
          two:
            - name: Bar
          three:
            name: Bar
```

### Changes > `linters` > `linters.enable[].<formatter_name>`

Source: https://github.com/golangci/golangci-lint/blob/main/docs/content/docs/product/migration-guide.md

The linters `gci`, `gofmt`, `gofumpt`, and `goimports`, previously listed under `linters.enable` in v1, have been moved to the `formatters.enable` section in v2.

--------------------------------

### Migration Guide > linters-settings

Source: https://github.com/golangci/golangci-lint/blob/main/docs/content/docs/product/migration-guide.md

The `linters-settings` section has been split into `linters.settings` and `formatters.settings`. Settings for `gci`, `gofmt`, `gofumpt`, and `goimports` are now located in `formatters.settings`.
```

> AGENT

Found it — two coupled issues in one: `golangci-lint-action@v6`'s "latest" resolves to golangci-lint **v1.64.8** (v6 predates v2), and that v1 binary was built with **go1.24**, which refuses our **go 1.25** module (`built ... lower than targeted Go version`). The fix is the **v8** action, which installs golangci-lint v2 (matching my v2 config) built with current Go:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_014DVRWUUF7tQXk6hEtNFUeK
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/.github/workflows/ci.yml",
  "old_string": "      - uses: golangci/golangci-lint-action@v6\n        with:\n          version: latest",
  "new_string": "      - uses: golangci/golangci-lint-action@v8\n        with:\n          version: latest"
}
```

CI is fully green — all four jobs pass **in parallel**:

```
run 27102561068: success
  Build: success   Vet: success   Test: success   Lint: success
```

## What "run in parallel" became
The CI workflow now has **4 independent jobs** (no `needs:` between them, so GitHub runs them concurrently), plus a `concurrency` group that cancels superseded runs on the same ref:

| Job | Does |
|---|---|
| **Build** | `go build ./...` (CGO-free, validates the static-binary build) |
| **Vet** | `go vet ./...` |
| **Test** | `go test -race` + coverage → Codecov upload (`CGO_ENABLED=1` for the race detector) |
| **Lint** | golangci-lint **v2** |

## Three real bugs caught and fixed along the way
1. **`-race` needs cgo** — the global `CGO_ENABLED=0` (kept so Build proves the cgo-free binary) broke the race detector; fixed with a job-level `CGO_ENABLED=1` on Test only.
2. **golangci-lint v2 config** — migrated `.golangci.yml` to the `version: "2"` schema (formatters split out), validated locally against golangci-lint v2.12.2.
3. **Action version** — `golangci-lint-action@v6` installed golangci-lint **v1** (built with go1.24, which refused our go 1.25 module); upgraded to **@v8** → installs v2.

All validated locally before each push (lint: 0 issues; `go test -race` passes).

## One thing still on you
The **Codecov badge/upload** needs the repo activated + token (I can't — it's your Codecov account):
```
! gh secret set CODECOV_TOKEN --repo ravencloak-org/caw
```
(after adding `caw` at codecov.io). CI stays green without it (`fail_ci_if_error: false`); the badge just reads "unknown" until the first upload.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/store/schema.sql",
  "old_string": "    summary     TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at  INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    PRIMARY KEY (owner, repo, number, signal_type)\n);",
  "new_string": "    summary     TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at  INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    PRIMARY KEY (owner, repo, number, signal_type)\n);\n\n-- Raw signals observed for a Round, compiled into a summary at settle time.\n-- Keyed by their natural identity so a re-delivered signal replaces, not dupes.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS signals (\n    owner       TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    repo        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    number      INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    sha         TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    signal_type TEXT    NOT NULL,           -- checks | comments | mergeability\n    source      TEXT    NOT NULL,           -- dynamic attribution, e.g. CI / CodeRabbit\n    external_id TEXT    NOT NULL,           -- per-source id for replace/dedupe\n    severity    TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    body        TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    updated_at  INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    PRIMARY KEY (owner, repo, number, sha, signal_type, source, external_id)\n);\n\n-- Hub-minted installation tokens for SSE / get_pending auth (ADR-0003).\n-- Only the hash is stored; the raw token is shown once at mint time.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tokens (\n    token_hash      TEXT PRIMARY KEY,        -- hex sha256 of the raw token\n    installation_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n    org             TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    created_at      INTEGER NOT NULL\n);"
}

  "new_string": "\t\titems = append(items, p)\n\t}\n\treturn items, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// Signal is one observed feedback item for a Round, awaiting compilation.\ntype Signal struct {\n\tOwner      string\n\tRepo       string\n\tNumber     int\n\tSHA        string\n\tSignalType string\n\tSource     string\n\tExternalID string\n\tSeverity   string\n\tBody       string\n\tUpdatedAt  int64\n}\n\n// AddSignal records a signal for a Round, replacing any prior signal with the\n// same natural key (owner/repo/number/sha + signal_type + source + external_id).\nfunc (s *Store) AddSignal(sig Signal) error {\n\t_, err := s.db.Exec(\n\t\t`INSERT INTO signals\n\t\t     (owner, repo, number, sha, signal_type, source, external_id, severity, body, updated_at)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(owner, repo, number, sha, signal_type, source, external_id) DO UPDATE SET\n\t\t     severity = excluded.severity, body = excluded.body, updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,\n\t\tsig.Owner, sig.Repo, sig.Number, sig.SHA, sig.SignalType,\n\t\tsig.Source, sig.ExternalID, sig.Severity, sig.Body, time.Now().Unix(),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"add signal: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// SignalsForRound returns every signal stored for a PR head SHA, in a stable order.\nfunc (s *Store) SignalsForRound(owner, repo string, number int, sha string) ([]Signal, error) {\n\trows, err := s.db.Query(\n\t\t`SELECT owner, repo, number, sha, signal_type, source, external_id, severity, body, updated_at\n\t\t FROM signals WHERE owner = ? AND repo = ? AND number = ? AND sha = ?\n\t\t ORDER BY signal_type, source, external_id`,\n\t\towner, repo, number, sha,\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"signals for round: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer func() { _ = rows.Close() }()\n\n\tvar out []Signal\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar sig Signal\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(\n\t\t\t&sig.Owner, &sig.Repo, &sig.Number, &sig.SHA, &sig.SignalType,\n\t\t\t&sig.Source, &sig.ExternalID, &sig.Severity, &sig.Body, &sig.UpdatedAt,\n\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"scan signal: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tout = append(out, sig)\n\t}\n\treturn out, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// InsertToken stores a hashed installation token (ADR-0003).\nfunc (s *Store) InsertToken(tokenHash, installationID, org string) error {\n\t_, err := s.db.Exec(\n\t\t`INSERT INTO tokens (token_hash, installation_id, org, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(token_hash) DO UPDATE SET installation_id = excluded.installation_id, org = excluded.org`,\n\t\ttokenHash, installationID, org, time.Now().Unix(),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"insert token: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// VerifyToken resolves a token hash to its installation id. ok is false when the\n// token is unknown. It satisfies the auth.Verifier interface.\nfunc (s *Store) VerifyToken(tokenHash string) (installationID string, ok bool, err error) {\n\terr = s.db.QueryRow(\n\t\t`SELECT installation_id FROM tokens WHERE token_hash = ?`, tokenHash,\n\t).Scan(&installationID)\n\tswitch {\n\tcase err == sql.ErrNoRows:\n\t\treturn \"\", false, nil\n\tcase err != nil:\n\t\treturn \"\", false, fmt.Errorf(\"verify token: %w\", err)\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn installationID, true, nil\n\t}\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/sse/hub.go",
  "content": "// Package sse implements the Hub's fan-out of compiled summaries to Watchers\n// over Server-Sent Events. Many Sessions may subscribe to one PR key; every\n// listener receives every summary (ADR-0007).\npackage sse\n\nimport \"sync\"\n\n// subBuffer is the per-subscriber channel depth. Summaries are infrequent, so a\n// small buffer absorbs brief reader stalls without blocking the publisher.\nconst subBuffer = 16\n\n// Subscriber is one live SSE connection bound to a PR key.\ntype Subscriber struct {\n\tC   chan []byte\n\tkey string\n}\n\n// Hub fans messages out to all subscribers of a key (owner/repo#number).\ntype Hub struct {\n\tmu   sync.RWMutex\n\tsubs map[string]map[*Subscriber]struct{}\n}\n\n// New returns an empty Hub.\nfunc New() *Hub {\n\treturn &Hub{subs: make(map[string]map[*Subscriber]struct{})}\n}\n\n// Subscribe registers and returns a Subscriber for key.\nfunc (h *Hub) Subscribe(key string) *Subscriber {\n\ts := &Subscriber{C: make(chan []byte, subBuffer), key: key}\n\th.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer h.mu.Unlock()\n\tif h.subs[key] == nil {\n\t\th.subs[key] = make(map[*Subscriber]struct{})\n\t}\n\th.subs[key][s] = struct{}{}\n\treturn s\n}\n\n// Unsubscribe removes s and closes its channel. Safe to call once per Subscriber.\nfunc (h *Hub) Unsubscribe(s *Subscriber) {\n\th.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer h.mu.Unlock()\n\tm := h.subs[s.key]\n\tif m == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif _, present := m[s]; !present {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdelete(m, s)\n\tif len(m) == 0 {\n\t\tdelete(h.subs, s.key)\n\t}\n\tclose(s.C)\n}\n\n// Publish delivers msg to every subscriber of key and returns how many received\n// it. A subscriber whose buffer is full is skipped (non-blocking) so one slow\n// reader cannot stall the publisher or other listeners.\nfunc (h *Hub) Publish(key string, msg []byte) int {\n\th.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer h.mu.RUnlock()\n\tdelivered := 0\n\tfor s := range h.subs[key] {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase s.C <- msg:\n\t\t\tdelivered++\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn delivered\n}\n\n// HasSubscribers reports whether key has at least one live subscriber.\n// Zero subscribers means the PR is orphaned (ADR-0006/0007).\nfunc (h *Hub) HasSubscribers(key string) bool {\n\th.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer h.mu.RUnlock()\n\treturn len(h.subs[key]) > 0\n}\n\n// CountSubscribers returns the number of live subscribers for key.\nfunc (h *Hub) CountSubscribers(key string) int {\n\th.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer h.mu.RUnlock()\n\treturn len(h.subs[key])\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package sse\n\nimport (\n\t\"github.com/gin-gonic/gin\"\n)\n\n// Handler returns a Gin handler that holds an SSE connection for the PR key\n// (derived from the request by keyFn) until the client disconnects, streaming\n// each published summary as a \"summary\" event.\n//\n// Buffering middleware must not wrap this route; the X-Accel-Buffering header\n// also tells a fronting proxy not to buffer (ADR-0001).\nfunc (h *Hub) Handler(keyFn func(*gin.Context) string) gin.HandlerFunc {\n\treturn func(c *gin.Context) {\n\t\tkey := keyFn(c)\n\t\tsub := h.Subscribe(key)\n\t\tdefer h.Unsubscribe(sub)\n\n\t\tc.Header(\"Content-Type\", \"text/event-stream\")\n\t\tc.Header(\"Cache-Control\", \"no-cache\")\n\t\tc.Header(\"Connection\", \"keep-alive\")\n\t\tc.Header(\"X-Accel-Buffering\", \"no\")\n\t\tc.Writer.Flush()\n\n\t\tctx := c.Request.Context()\n\t\tc.Stream(func(_ io.Writer) bool {\n\t\t\tselect {\n\t\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\tcase msg, ok := <-sub.C:\n\t\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tc.SSEvent(\"summary\", string(msg))\n\t\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/sse/hub_test.go",
  "content": "package sse\n\nimport \"testing\"\n\nfunc TestPublishFanOut(t *testing.T) {\n\th := New()\n\ta := h.Subscribe(\"o/r#1\")\n\tb := h.Subscribe(\"o/r#1\")\n\tother := h.Subscribe(\"o/r#2\")\n\n\tif got := h.CountSubscribers(\"o/r#1\"); got != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"CountSubscribers = %d, want 2\", got)\n\t}\n\tif !h.HasSubscribers(\"o/r#1\") {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"HasSubscribers should be true\")\n\t}\n\n\tn := h.Publish(\"o/r#1\", []byte(\"hello\"))\n\tif n != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Publish delivered to %d, want 2\", n)\n\t}\n\tfor _, s := range []*Subscriber{a, b} {\n\t\tif got := <-s.C; string(got) != \"hello\" {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"subscriber got %q, want hello\", got)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tselect {\n\tcase msg := <-other.C:\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"subscriber on other key received %q\", msg)\n\tdefault:\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUnsubscribe(t *testing.T) {\n\th := New()\n\ts := h.Subscribe(\"k\")\n\th.Unsubscribe(s)\n\n\tif h.HasSubscribers(\"k\") {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"HasSubscribers should be false after unsubscribe\")\n\t}\n\tif n := h.Publish(\"k\", []byte(\"x\")); n != 0 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Publish after unsubscribe delivered %d, want 0\", n)\n\t}\n\tif _, ok := <-s.C; ok {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"subscriber channel should be closed after unsubscribe\")\n\t}\n\t// Second Unsubscribe must be a safe no-op.\n\th.Unsubscribe(s)\n}\n\nfunc TestPublishNoSubscribers(t *testing.T) {\n\th := New()\n\tif n := h.Publish(\"nobody\", []byte(\"x\")); n != 0 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Publish with no subscribers delivered %d, want 0\", n)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestPublishSkipsFullBuffer(t *testing.T) {\n\th := New()\n\ts := h.Subscribe(\"k\")\n\t// Fill the buffer without draining.\n\tfor i := 0; i < subBuffer; i++ {\n\t\tif n := h.Publish(\"k\", []byte(\"x\")); n != 1 {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"buffered publish %d delivered %d, want 1\", i, n)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// Next publish must be dropped (non-blocking), not block the publisher.\n\tif n := h.Publish(\"k\", []byte(\"overflow\")); n != 0 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"overflow publish delivered %d, want 0 (dropped)\", n)\n\t}\n\t_ = s\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/compile/compile.go",
  "content": "// Package compile turns a Round's stored signals into the single summary that\n// is pushed per settle (ADR-0004). Sources are attributed dynamically; the\n// signal-type ladder is fixed (checks, comments, mergeability).\npackage compile\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"sort\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/store\"\n)\n\n// Item is one signal within a group.\ntype Item struct {\n\tSource   string `json:\"source\"`\n\tSeverity string `json:\"severity,omitempty\"`\n\tBody     string `json:\"body,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// SignalGroup collects all signals of one signal-type for a Round.\ntype SignalGroup struct {\n\tType    string   `json:\"type\"`\n\tSources []string `json:\"sources\"`\n\tCount   int      `json:\"count\"`\n\tItems   []Item   `json:\"items\"`\n}\n\n// Summary is the compiled, serializable artifact for one settle of a Round.\ntype Summary struct {\n\tKey    string        `json:\"key\"` // owner/repo#number@sha\n\tSeq    int           `json:\"seq\"` // monotonic per Round (ADR-0004)\n\tGroups []SignalGroup `json:\"groups\"`\n\tText   string        `json:\"text\"`\n}\n\n// typeOrder fixes the rendering order of the signal-type ladder.\nvar typeOrder = map[string]int{\"checks\": 0, \"comments\": 1, \"mergeability\": 2}\n\n// Compile groups signals by signal-type into one Summary tagged with seq.\nfunc Compile(key string, seq int, signals []store.Signal) Summary {\n\tgroups := make(map[string]*SignalGroup)\n\tsources := make(map[string]map[string]struct{})\n\n\tfor _, sig := range signals {\n\t\tg := groups[sig.SignalType]\n\t\tif g == nil {\n\t\t\tg = &SignalGroup{Type: sig.SignalType}\n\t\t\tgroups[sig.SignalType] = g\n\t\t\tsources[sig.SignalType] = make(map[string]struct{})\n\t\t}\n\t\tg.Count++\n\t\tg.Items = append(g.Items, Item{Source: sig.Source, Severity: sig.Severity, Body: sig.Body})\n\t\tif sig.Source != \"\" {\n\t\t\tsources[sig.SignalType][sig.Source] = struct{}{}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tout := make([]SignalGroup, 0, len(groups))\n\tfor t, g := range groups {\n\t\tfor s := range sources[t] {\n\t\t\tg.Sources = append(g.Sources, s)\n\t\t}\n\t\tsort.Strings(g.Sources)\n\t\tout = append(out, *g)\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\toi, oj, oki := typeOrder[out[i].Type], typeOrder[out[j].Type], false\n\t\t_, oki = typeOrder[out[i].Type]\n\t\t_, okj := typeOrder[out[j].Type]\n\t\t// Known types first in ladder order; unknown types after, alphabetical.\n\t\tif oki != okj {\n\t\t\treturn oki\n\t\t}\n\t\tif oi != oj {\n\t\t\treturn oi < oj\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn out[i].Type < out[j].Type\n\t})\n\n\treturn Summary{Key: key, Seq: seq, Groups: out, Text: render(key, seq, out)}\n}\n\nfunc render(key string, seq int, groups []SignalGroup) string {\n\tif len(groups) == 0 {\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s (seq %d): no signals\", key, seq)\n\t}\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"%s (seq %d)\", key, seq)\n\tfor _, g := range groups {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"\\n- %s: %d\", g.Type, g.Count)\n\t\tif len(g.Sources) > 0 {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \" [%s]\", strings.Join(g.Sources, \", \"))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/settle/settle.go",
  "content": "// Package settle schedules Round settles: a grace window after a trigger,\n// re-armed by late same-SHA signals (re-settle, ADR-0004). On fire it compiles\n// the Round's signals into one summary and fans it out, or stores it as pending\n// when no Session is listening (ADR-0006).\npackage settle\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/compile\"\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/store\"\n)\n\n// DefaultGrace is the settle grace window after the latest trigger.\nconst DefaultGrace = 30 * time.Second\n\n// Publisher fans a serialized summary out to subscribers of a PR key.\ntype Publisher interface {\n\tPublish(key string, msg []byte) int\n}\n\n// Engine schedules and fires Round settles.\ntype Engine struct {\n\tstore *store.Store\n\tpub   Publisher\n\tgrace time.Duration\n\n\tmu     sync.Mutex\n\trounds map[string]*roundState\n}\n\ntype roundState struct {\n\towner, repo string\n\tnumber      int\n\tsha         string\n\ttimer       *time.Timer\n\tseq         int\n}\n\n// New builds an Engine. A non-positive grace falls back to DefaultGrace.\nfunc New(st *store.Store, pub Publisher, grace time.Duration) *Engine {\n\tif grace <= 0 {\n\t\tgrace = DefaultGrace\n\t}\n\treturn &Engine{store: st, pub: pub, grace: grace, rounds: make(map[string]*roundState)}\n}\n\nfunc roundKey(owner, repo string, number int, sha string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s#%d@%s\", owner, repo, number, sha)\n}\n\n// PRKey is the SSE subscription key for a PR (owner/repo#number), independent of SHA.\nfunc PRKey(owner, repo string, number int) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s#%d\", owner, repo, number)\n}\n\n// Touch (re)arms the grace timer for a Round. The first call opens the settle;\n// a later same-SHA call re-settles it (ADR-0004).\nfunc (e *Engine) Touch(owner, repo string, number int, sha string) {\n\trk := roundKey(owner, repo, number, sha)\n\te.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer e.mu.Unlock()\n\trs := e.rounds[rk]\n\tif rs == nil {\n\t\trs = &roundState{owner: owner, repo: repo, number: number, sha: sha}\n\t\te.rounds[rk] = rs\n\t}\n\tif rs.timer != nil {\n\t\trs.timer.Stop()\n\t}\n\trs.timer = time.AfterFunc(e.grace, func() { e.fire(rk) })\n}\n\n// FireNow settles a Round immediately, bypassing the grace timer.\nfunc (e *Engine) FireNow(owner, repo string, number int, sha string) {\n\trk := roundKey(owner, repo, number, sha)\n\te.mu.Lock()\n\trs := e.rounds[rk]\n\tif rs == nil {\n\t\trs = &roundState{owner: owner, repo: repo, number: number, sha: sha}\n\t\te.rounds[rk] = rs\n\t}\n\tif rs.timer != nil {\n\t\trs.timer.Stop()\n\t}\n\te.mu.Unlock()\n\te.fire(rk)\n}\n\n// fire compiles and dispatches a settle for the given Round.\nfunc (e *Engine) fire(rk string) {\n\te.mu.Lock()\n\trs := e.rounds[rk]\n\tif rs == nil {\n\t\te.mu.Unlock()\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\trs.seq++\n\tseq := rs.seq\n\towner, repo, number, sha := rs.owner, rs.repo, rs.number, rs.sha\n\te.mu.Unlock()\n\n\tsignals, err := e.store.SignalsForRound(owner, repo, number, sha)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"settle %s: load signals: %v\", rk, err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tsummary := compile.Compile(rk, seq, signals)\n\tmsg, err := json.Marshal(summary)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"settle %s: marshal: %v\", rk, err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif e.pub.Publish(PRKey(owner, repo, number), msg) == 0 {\n\t\t// Orphaned: no live Session. Persist as pending, latest per signal-type.\n\t\tfor _, g := range summary.Groups {\n\t\t\tif err := e.store.UpsertPending(store.PendingItem{\n\t\t\t\tOwner: owner, Repo: repo, Number: number,\n\t\t\t\tSignalType: g.Type, SHA: sha, PRState: \"open\", Summary: string(msg),\n\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"settle %s: pend %s: %v\", rk, g.Type, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package compile\n\nimport (\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/store\"\n)\n\nfunc sig(typ, src, sev, body string) store.Signal {\n\treturn store.Signal{\n\t\tOwner: \"o\", Repo: \"r\", Number: 1, SHA: \"sha\",\n\t\tSignalType: typ, Source: src, Severity: sev, Body: body,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestCompileGroupsByTypeWithDynamicSources(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := Compile(\"o/r#1@sha\", 2, []store.Signal{\n\t\tsig(\"comments\", \"CodeRabbit\", \"MAJOR\", \"nit\"),\n\t\tsig(\"checks\", \"CI\", \"\", \"lint failed\"),\n\t\tsig(\"checks\", \"CI\", \"\", \"vet failed\"),\n\t\tsig(\"comments\", \"human\", \"\", \"lgtm?\"),\n\t})\n\n\tif s.Key != \"o/r#1@sha\" || s.Seq != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"key/seq wrong: key=%q seq=%d\", s.Key, s.Seq)\n\t}\n\tif len(s.Groups) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"groups = %d, want 2\", len(s.Groups))\n\t}\n\tif s.Groups[0].Type != \"checks\" || s.Groups[1].Type != \"comments\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ladder order wrong: %s then %s\", s.Groups[0].Type, s.Groups[1].Type)\n\t}\n\tif s.Groups[0].Count != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"checks count = %d, want 2\", s.Groups[0].Count)\n\t}\n\tif got := s.Groups[0].Sources; len(got) != 1 || got[0] != \"CI\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"checks sources = %v, want [CI] (deduped)\", got)\n\t}\n\tif got := s.Groups[1].Sources; len(got) != 2 || got[0] != \"CodeRabbit\" || got[1] != \"human\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"comments sources = %v, want sorted [CodeRabbit human]\", got)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestCompileEmpty(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := Compile(\"k\", 1, nil)\n\tif len(s.Groups) != 0 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"groups = %d, want 0\", len(s.Groups))\n\t}\n\tif !strings.Contains(s.Text, \"no signals\") {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"text = %q\", s.Text)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestCompileTextRendering(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := Compile(\"o/r#1@sha\", 1, []store.Signal{sig(\"checks\", \"CI\", \"\", \"x\")})\n\tif !strings.Contains(s.Text, \"checks: 1\") || !strings.Contains(s.Text, \"[CI]\") {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"text = %q\", s.Text)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestCompileUnknownTypeOrderedAfterKnown(t *testing.T) {\n\ts := Compile(\"k\", 1, []store.Signal{\n\t\tsig(\"zzz\", \"x\", \"\", \"\"),\n\t\tsig(\"checks\", \"CI\", \"\", \"\"),\n\t\tsig(\"mergeability\", \"poll\", \"\", \"\"),\n\t})\n\tif s.Groups[0].Type != \"checks\" || s.Groups[1].Type != \"mergeability\" || s.Groups[2].Type != \"zzz\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"order wrong: %s, %s, %s\", s.Groups[0].Type, s.Groups[1].Type, s.Groups[2].Type)\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package settle\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/ravencloak-org/caw/internal/store\"\n)\n\ntype fakePub struct {\n\tmu       sync.Mutex\n\tcount    int // value Publish returns (simulated subscriber count)\n\tmessages [][]byte\n}\n\nfunc (f *fakePub) Publish(_ string, msg []byte) int {\n\tf.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer f.mu.Unlock()\n\tf.messages = append(f.messages, msg)\n\treturn f.count\n}\n\nfunc (f *fakePub) n() int {\n\tf.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer f.mu.Unlock()\n\treturn len(f.messages)\n}\n\nfunc (f *fakePub) last() []byte {\n\tf.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer f.mu.Unlock()\n\treturn f.messages[len(f.messages)-1]\n}\n\nfunc newStore(t *testing.T) *store.Store {\n\tt.Helper()\n\ts, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), \"s.db\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"open store: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tt.Cleanup(func() { _ = s.Close() })\n\treturn s\n}\n\nfunc seed(t *testing.T, st *store.Store) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tif err := st.AddSignal(store.Signal{\n\t\tOwner: \"o\", Repo: \"r\", Number: 1, SHA: \"sha\",\n\t\tSignalType: \"checks\", Source: \"CI\", ExternalID: \"1\", Body: \"fail\",\n\t}); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"seed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestSettleFiresAfterGrace(t *testing.T) {\n\tst := newStore(t)\n\tseed(t, st)\n\tpub := &fakePub{count: 1}\n\te := New(st, pub, 20*time.Millisecond)\n\n\te.Touch(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha\")\n\tif pub.n() != 0 { // timers never fire before their deadline\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"settled before grace elapsed\")\n\t}\n\ttime.Sleep(120 * time.Millisecond)\n\tif pub.n() != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"messages = %d, want 1\", pub.n())\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestReSettleReArmsTimer(t *testing.T) {\n\tst := newStore(t)\n\tseed(t, st)\n\tpub := &fakePub{count: 1}\n\te := New(st, pub, 50*time.Millisecond)\n\n\te.Touch(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha\") // deadline ~t+50\n\ttime.Sleep(30 * time.Millisecond)\n\te.Touch(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha\") // re-arm: deadline ~t+80\n\ttime.Sleep(35 * time.Millisecond)\n\tif pub.n() != 0 { // ~t+65 < t+80: cannot have fired yet\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"re-settle fired early: %d\", pub.n())\n\t}\n\ttime.Sleep(120 * time.Millisecond)\n\tif pub.n() != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"messages = %d, want 1\", pub.n())\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestOrphanStoresPending(t *testing.T) {\n\tst := newStore(t)\n\tseed(t, st)\n\tpub := &fakePub{count: 0} // no subscribers\n\te := New(st, pub, time.Millisecond)\n\n\te.FireNow(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha\")\n\n\titems, err := st.ListPending()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ListPending: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(items) != 1 || items[0].SignalType != \"checks\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"pending = %+v, want one checks item\", items)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestSeqIncrementsAcrossSettles(t *testing.T) {\n\tst := newStore(t)\n\tseed(t, st)\n\tpub := &fakePub{count: 1}\n\te := New(st, pub, time.Millisecond)\n\n\te.FireNow(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha\")\n\te.FireNow(\"o\", \"r\", 1, \"sha\")\n\tif pub.n() != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"messages = %d, want 2\", pub.n())\n\t}\n\tvar got struct {\n\t\tSeq int `json:\"seq\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(pub.last(), &got); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unmarshal: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif got.Seq != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"seq = %d, want 2\", got.Seq)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestPRKey(t *testing.T) {\n\tif got := PRKey(\"o\", \"r\", 7); got != \"o/r#7\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"PRKey = %q\", got)\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/caw/internal/github/events.go",
  "content": "// Package github models the subset of GitHub webhook payloads the Hub needs.\npackage github\n\n// Repository identifies the repo an event belongs to.\ntype Repository struct {\n\tName  string `json:\"name\"`\n\tOwner struct {\n\t\tLogin string `json:\"login\"`\n\t} `json:\"owner\"`\n}\n\n// PullRequest carries the PR number, state, and head SHA.\ntype PullRequest struct {\n\tNumber int    `json:\"number\"`\n\tState  string `json:\"state\"`\n\tHead   struct {\n\t\tSHA string `json:\"sha\"`\n\t} `json:\"head\"`\n}\n\n// CheckSuitePR is a PR reference inside a check_suite payload.\ntype CheckSuitePR struct {\n\tNumber int `json:\"number\"`\n}\n\n// CheckSuite is a check_suite event payload (the settle trigger).\ntype CheckSuite struct {\n\tID           int64          `json:\"id\"`\n\tHeadSHA      string         `json:\"head_sha\"`\n\tStatus       string         `json:\"status\"`\n\tConclusion   string         `json:\"conclusion\"`\n\tApp          struct{ Name string `json:\"name\"` } `json:\"app\"`\n\tPullRequests []CheckSuitePR `json:\"pull_requests\"`\n}\n\n// Comment is an issue_comment or review-comment body.\ntype Comment struct {\n\tID   int64  `json:\"id\"`\n\tBody string `json:\"body\"`\n}\n\n// Review is a pull_request_review payload.\ntype Review struct {\n\tID    int64  `json:\"id\"`\n\tBody  string `json:\"body\"`\n\tState string `json:\"state\"`\n}\n\n// Issue carries the number for issue_comment events (PRs are issues).\ntype Issue struct {\n\tNumber int `json:\"number\"`\n}\n\n// Envelope captures the fields required to bucket a webhook into a Round and to\n// extract signals. Optional pointers reflect which payloads carry which objects.\ntype Envelope struct {\n\tAction      string       `json:\"action\"`\n\tNumber      int          `json:\"number\"`\n\tRepository  Repository   `json:\"repository\"`\n\tPullRequest *PullRequest `json:\"pull_request\"`\n\tCheckSuite  *CheckSuite  `json:\"check_suite\"`\n\tComment     *Comment     `json:\"comment\"`\n\tReview      *Review      `json:\"review\"`\n\tIssue       *Issue       `json:\"issue\"`\n\tSender      struct {\n\t\tLogin string `json:\"login\"`\n\t} `json:\"sender\"`\n}\n"
}
